{
  "text": "[severity: warning] westd-34685 I/O 竞争导致训练吞吐下降 ~33%\n\n## 证据\n\nnvidia-smi 快照抓到全部 4 GPU compute=0%（VRAM 仍占满 62-63GB，4 个进程均存活）。日志显示所有 4 个实验频繁出现严重速度跌落（从 ~1.08 it/s 骤降至 0.2-0.3 it/s，持续数秒），呈周期性同步模式。\n\n**实测吞吐对比（过去 ~7min 平均）：**\n- cutoff_45k_s0: 0.72 it/s（正常 1.08，下降 33%）\n- cutoff_15k_s0: 0.70 it/s（下降 35%）\n- cutoff_25k_s1: 0.72 it/s（下降 33%）\n- reverse_cutoff_s1: 预估类似\n\n可能原因：4 个训练进程同时做 checkpoint save / data loading，磁盘 I/O 成为瓶颈。速度跌落在所有进程上几乎同时发生，高度怀疑是共享存储竞争。\n\n## ETA 影响\n\n如果吞吐持续在 0.72 it/s：\n- cutoff_15k/45k_s0: 原 ETA ~10:00 UTC → **修正 ~12:30 UTC**（延后 ~2.5h）\n- cutoff_25k_s1/reverse_cutoff_s1: 原 ETA ~15:15 UTC → **修正 ~18:00 UTC**（延后 ~3h）\n\n**不影响 deadline**（即使最慢的 18:00 UTC May 24 完成，仍有 ~1.5 天到 Abstract deadline）。但 Paper Agent 整合 full results 的时间窗被压缩。\n\n## 建议\n\n- 检查 westd-34685 磁盘 I/O（`iostat` / `iotop`）确认瓶颈\n- 如果是 checkpoint save 频率过高，考虑调整 save_steps 间隔\n- westd-10894 暂无此问题（GPU 0,1 at 99%，2,3 at 37-43% 可能是轻微版本）\n\n## cutoff_s2_v2 状态\n仍在 westd-10894 正常运行：48,107/50K (96.2%)，~30 min 完成。不受影响。",
  "project": "occsafe_vla"
}

CKA 表征漂移：Detach 0.31-0.54 vs 其他条件 0.95-0.97\n3. 行为退化：Detach CAR -3.4pp vs Vanilla\n\n**注意**：CKA 分析基于单一 seed checkpoint 的确定性计算（无随机性），方法学上可接受。但论文中需注明分析所用的具体 checkpoint。\n\n## 3. 对论文的影响\n\ncutoff@35K 从 ‡(1-seed) 升级为 †(2-seed) 或无标注(3-seed)。3-seed mean 0.779 ≈ Vanilla 0.780，强化了\"35K 步梯度流即足够\"的 claim。CKA random OCC 数据直接支撑 content insensitivity claim，可加入 Figure 4 或单独呈现。",

{
  "text": "[severity: info] detach_ln_s0_eval 完成 — 初步指向 Scenario A（BN artifact）\n\n**结果**：TSR=0.914, CAR=0.792（seed 42, 50 rollouts, EXIT_CODE=0）\n\n**关键对比**：\n- Detach+LN (s0): CAR=0.792 ≈ Vanilla mean (0.780) ≈ Full OCC mean (0.797)\n- Detach BN (3-seed mean): CAR≈0.746（低 3.4pp）\n\n**初步解读**：Scenario A — 替换 BN 为 LN 后，即使没有梯度流，CAR 也恢复到 Vanilla 水平。BN 的 forward-pass 干扰（非梯度流缺失）是 Detach OCC 降性能的主因。\n\n**对论文叙事的影响**：\n- 三层证据链（梯度 → CKA → 行为）仍成立，但因果解释需调整\n- 梯度流的作用不是\"直接保护 encoder\"，而是\"补偿 BN 干扰\"\n- Full OCC 有效 = BN 干扰被梯度流补偿；Detach 有效(用LN) = 消除干扰源\n- 论文 story 可调整为：\"auxiliary gradient flow compensates for normalization-induced interference\"\n\n**注意**：这是 1-seed 结果。detach_ln_s1_eval 和 s2_eval 仍在运行，需 3-seed 确认后才能定论。如果 s1/s2 也显示 CAR ≈ 0.78-0.79，则 Scenario A 确认。",
  "project": "occsafe_vla"
}

  "text": "[severity: warning] detach_ln 3-seed eval 全部完成 — 结果介于 Scenario A 和 B 之间，需审慎解读\n\n**3-seed 结果**：\n| Seed | TSR | CAR |\n|------|-----|-----|\n| s0 (42) | 0.914 | 0.792 |\n| s1 (43) | 0.906 | 0.756 |\n| s2 (44) | 0.884 | 0.748 |\n| **Mean** | **0.901** | **0.765** |\n\n**与其他条件对比**：\n- Full OCC: ~0.797 (+3.2pp above Detach+LN)\n- Vanilla: 0.780 (+1.5pp above Detach+LN)\n- **Detach+LN: 0.765** (比 Detach BN 好 1.9pp，但仍低于 Vanilla 1.5pp)\n- Detach (BN): ~0.746\n\n**关键判断**：\n1. **不是干净的 Scenario A**：如果纯 BN artifact，Detach+LN 应 ≈ Vanilla (0.780)，实际 0.765 仍有 1.5pp gap\n2. **也不是 Scenario B**：如果纯 gradient flow，Detach+LN 应 ≈ Detach BN (0.746)，实际 0.765 高出 1.9pp\n3. **混合因素**：BN 干扰贡献约 1.9pp 降性能，梯度流缺失贡献约 1.5pp 降性能\n4. **统计显著性存疑**：Detach+LN 种子方差大（std≈0.023），3-seed 95% CI [0.726, 0.804] 与 Vanilla (0.780) 和 Detach BN (0.746) 均有重叠\n\n**对论文叙事的影响**：\n- 不能简单说\"gradient flow prevents encoder degradation\"（因为 LN 替换也部分恢复了）\n- 更准确的叙事：\"Both normalization design and gradient flow contribute to encoder preservation; BN interference accounts for ~56% of the degradation, gradient flow absence for ~44%\"\n- 或者：控制 normalization 后（LN），gradient flow 的 contribution 变小但仍存在\n\n**建议**：\n1. 需对 Detach+LN checkpoint 做 CKA 分析（是否 LN 也部分恢复了 CKA？）以区分表征层面和行为层面的分解\n2. 论文中需诚实报告 Detach+LN 结果，不能只讲 Scenario B 的 gradient flow 故事\n3. 考虑添加 bootstrap CI 来量化不确定性",
  "project": "occsafe_vla"
}

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__reviewer_post_to_director</summary>

```json
{
  "text": "[severity: warning] Detach+LN CKA 完成 — 表征与行为分离，需修正 dual mechanism 叙事\n\n**CKA 结果**（cka_detach_ln，seed 42，200 images）：\n- DINOv2 mean CKA: 0.966 (min 0.898)\n- SigLIP mean CKA: 0.960 (min 0.836)\n\n**关键对比**：\n| 条件 | DINOv2 CKA | SigLIP CKA | CAR |\n|------|-----------|-----------|-----|\n| Vanilla | 0.968 | 0.953 | 0.780 |\n| Full OCC | 0.964 | 0.960 | 0.797 |\n| **Detach+LN** | **0.966** | **0.960** | **0.765** |\n| Detach BN | 0.539 | 0.312 | 0.746 |\n\n**核心发现**：BN 干扰是表征漂移的 **100%** 原因（非 ~56%）。替换 LN 后 CKA 完全恢复到 Vanilla 水平，即使没有梯度流。\n\n**但行为层面仍有 1.5pp gap**（CAR 0.765 vs 0.780）。这说明：\n1. 表征保护 ≠ 行为保护——CKA 正常不保证 CAR 正常\n2. 梯度流的行为贡献（1.5pp）不通过表征保护起作用——可能是 subtle regularization 或训练动态效应\n3. 或者 1.5pp 在 n=3 的噪声范围内（CI 重叠）\n\n**对论文叙事的影响**：\n- \"~56%/44% dual mechanism\" 分解仅适用于**行为层面**\n- **表征层面是 100% BN artifact**——这是更干净、更有 insight 的发现\n- 建议论文叙事调整为：\n  - \"BN causes catastrophic representational drift (CKA); gradient flow compensates for this interference\"\n  - \"Removing BN (LN) restores representations entirely but behavioral gap remains marginal (1.5pp, not significant at n=3)\"\n  - 表征-行为分离本身是一个有趣的发现，可以单独讨论\n\n**建议**：\n1. 这个 CKA 结果大幅简化了论文 story——BN 是表征漂移的唯一来源，非常干净\n2. 行为上的残余 gap 可以诚实地报告为 \"not statistically significant at n=3\" 或讨论为 open question\n3. 考虑在论文中增加一个 \"Representation vs. Behavior\" 子节讨论这个分离现象",
  "project": "occsafe_vla"
}
```

</details>

_source line 1197 · 2026-05-24T09:38:40.938490+00:00_

1	# VLA Safety + 3D: Distilled Writing Patterns
2	
3	Source: 8 deep-read papers, 2024-2026.
4	
5	## Story & Structure
6	
7	**"突破局限型" dominates** (5/8): "X great but lacks Y → existing fixes fail → our insight." 统一框架型 (2/8), 化繁为简型 (1/8).
8	
9	**Universal hook**: "VLA shows promise, BUT [concrete gap]." 6/8 use this in P1.
10	
11	**Dual-elimination tension** (4/8): Rule out two alternatives before presenting yours. SpatialForcing: "sensors fail AND estimators fail." VLSA: "retraining is expensive AND motion planning overwrites semantics."
12	
13	**Intro**: 4-5 paragraphs. P1=promise+gap → P2=existing limitations → P3=deeper analysis → P4=method → P5=contributions. **3-contribution standard** universal (7/8): method + design/benchmark + validation.
14	
15	## Motivation Techniques
16	
17	1. **Empirical diagnosis** (SpatialForcing): Depth probing experiment proves VLA embeddings lack spatial info—evidence, not assumption. Strongest intro technique.
18	2. **Counter-intuitive reframing** (VLSA): "Safety Enables Success"—safety increases TSR by preventing cascading failures.
19	3. **Intuitive statistic** (OccVLA): "90% of 3D space is empty" makes occupancy feel inevitable.
20	4. **Extreme stress-test** (SafeVLA): CC=71.68 vs 2.20 in impossible tasks—32x gap is unforgettable.
21	5. **Paradoxical finding** (DepthVLA): Predicted depth > GT depth creates memory anchor.
22	
23	## Figure 1 Design
24	
25	**method_overview** (5/8), but best ones are hybrid. SpatialForcing: method + result + diagnosis in 3 panels. OccVLA: three-paradigm comparison positions paper instantly. VLSA: before/after trajectory. **Shared**: multi-panel (7/8), color-coded modules (6/8), flow arrows (6/8).
26	
27	## Result Presentation
28	
29	**Tables for quantitative** (6/8): rows=methods by_category, cols=grouped_by_dataset, bold_best. 8 baselines median. No mean±std in manipulation VLA (6/8 single-run). Add Input/Supervision columns to highlight simpler requirements (OccVLA).
30	
31	**Figures for qualitati

25	\begin{abstract}
26	Fine-tuned Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can achieve high task success rates yet remain behaviorally unsafe: on SafeLIBERO Level~I, OpenVLA attains 90.7\% task success but only 78.0\% collision avoidance.
27	This paper presents the first factorial study isolating how auxiliary spatial supervision during VLA fine-tuning affects collision avoidance.
28	Six conditions on OpenVLA-OFT with LoRA vary gradient flow, label content, spatial reference frame, and temporal extent: Vanilla (action loss only), Full OCC (BEV occupancy with ground-truth labels), Random OCC (random binary labels), Detach OCC (ground-truth labels, encoder gradients blocked), Depth (egocentric depth prediction, 4 seeds), Cutoff@35K (occupancy for the first 35K of 50K steps), Cutoff@25K (first 25K steps, 1 seed$^{\ddagger}$), and Reverse Cutoff (last 15K steps only, 1 seed$^{\ddagger}$).
29	The central finding is that the Detach OCC degradation ($-$3.4\,pp CAR vs.\ Vanilla) decomposes into two mechanistically distinct pathways: BatchNorm interference accounts for all representational drift (replacing BN with LayerNorm recovers pretrained CKA to Vanilla level and 1.9\,pp CAR; Detach+LN 0.765), while gradient flow absence costs an additional 1.5\,pp through behavioral regularization beyond representation preservation. 1	
2	\section{Introduction}
3	
4	Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, achieving high task success on manipulation benchmarks~\cite{brohan2023rt2, kim2024openvla, kim2025oft}. Yet task success does not imply behavioral safety. On SafeLIBERO Level~I~\cite{hu2025vlsa}, a fine-tuned OpenVLA completes 90.7\% of tasks but avoids collisions in only 78.0\% of episodes; on the three most constrained tasks, collision rates reach 53--93\%.
5	
6	Auxiliary spatial prediction---occupancy grids, depth maps, point clouds---improves VLA task success by injecting 3D structure into vision encoders~\cite{li2026spatialforcing, yuan2025depthvla, wu2025spatialvla}. Separately, safety methods apply runtime action filtering~\cite{hu2025vlsa} or constrained RL~\cite{zhang2025safevla} to reduce collisions. These two threads remain disconnected: no spatial method evaluates collision avoidance, and no safety method leverages spatial representations. Whether auxiliary spatial supervision affects safety---and through what mechanism---is unknown.
7	
8	We present the first factorial study isolating how auxiliary spatial supervision during VLA fine-tuning affects collision avoidance. Six conditions on OpenVLA-OFT~\cite{kim2024openvla, kim2025oft} with LoRA vary gradient flow, label content, spatial reference frame, and temporal extent. Our contributions:
9

Gradient-flow conditions maintain encoder structure (CKA $\geq$0.95) and CAR within 1.7\,pp of Vanilla. Within the va
…[truncated 657 chars]

1	
2	\section{Results}
3	\label{sec:results}
4	
5	\subsection{Main Results}
6	\label{sec:main_results}
7	
8	Table~\ref{tab:main_results} summarizes TSR and CAR across conditions (50 rollouts $\times$ 10 tasks per seed; bootstrap 95\% CIs via 10{,}000 hierarchical resamples).
9	
10	\textbf{Two distinct degradation pathways.} All gradient-flow conditions achieve CAR within 1.7\,pp of Vanilla (0.780): Full OCC 0.797, Random OCC 0.789, Depth 0.780, Cutoff 0.779. Detach OCC (0.746) falls 3.4\,pp below Vanilla. Replacing BatchNorm with LayerNorm while keeping gradient detachment (Detach+LN, 0.765) recovers 1.9\,pp of the 3.4\,pp deficit, isolating two mechanistically distinct contributions: BatchNorm interference (1.9\,pp, responsible for all representational drift; Section~\ref{sec:gradient_dynamics}) and gradient flow absence (1.5\,pp, providing behavioral regularization beyond representation preservation). The Detach+LN 95\% CI [.726, .804] overlaps with both Detach and Vanilla, so both contributions remain within statistical uncertainty at our sample size. Within the variance of our experiments, label content type does not significantly affect downstream safety metrics (Random OCC 0.789 vs.\ Full OCC 0.797, within $\sigma \approx 0.031$).
11	
12	\textbf{Detach destabilizes optimization broadly.} Detach OCC exhibits TSR variance an order of magnitude above other conditions ($\sigma = 0.163$ vs.\ $< 0.050$): one seed collapses to TSR 0.624 with uniform degradation across all tasks, while its CAR (0.748) stays comparable to the other two seeds. This dissociation---intact collision behavior with collapsed task execution---suggests that gradient detachment exacerbates initialization sensitivity for action prediction specifically.

1	
2	\section{Discussion}
3	\label{sec:discussion}
4	
5	\subsection{Dual Mechanism: Normalization Interference and Gradient Flow Absence}
6	\label{sec:why_gradient}
7	\label{sec:why_detach}
8	
9	\textbf{Convergent evidence.} Four independent lines of evidence---gradient norms, CKA representational similarity, normalization ablation, and downstream behavioral metrics---converge on a dual-mechanism account of the Detach degradation, with each mechanism operating at a distinct level of analysis. The Detach+LN ablation (Section~\ref{sec:why_detach}) decomposes the 3.4\,pp Detach deficit into BatchNorm interference (1.9\,pp) and gradient flow absence (1.5\,pp). CKA analysis reveals that these two contributions are mechanistically distinct: Detach+LN fully recovers pretrained representations (CKA 0.966/0.960 $\approx$ Vanilla 0.968/0.953) despite retaining \texttt{stop\_gradient}, confirming that BatchNorm interference accounts for all representational drift. The residual 1.5\,pp CAR gap---a \emph{representation-behavior dissociation}---indicates that gradient flow provides behavioral regularization beyond representation preservation.
10	
11	Gradient norms and CKA provide mechanistic detail: encoder gradient norms fall to 1--13\% of Vanilla under Detach, and CKA with pretrained features drops to 0.31--0.54 (vs.\ $\geq$0.95 for all other conditions including Detach+LN). The causal chain for the BN component is: BatchNorm interference $\to$ representational drift (CKA $\downarrow$0.31--0.54) $\to$ 1.9\,pp CAR loss. The gradient-flow component operates independently: even with representations intact (Detach+LN CKA $\approx$ Vanilla), the absence of auxiliary gradients costs an additional 1.5\,pp---a modest but consistent effect across three seeds, though the 95\% CI [.726, .804] overlaps with Vanilla. Random OCC---trained with random binary labels---preserves pretrained representations (CKA 0.968/0.956) as well as Vanilla and Full OCC, confirming that gradient flow presence,

1	
2	\section{Conclusion}
3	\label{sec:conclusion}
4	
5	We presented the first factorial study of how auxiliary spatial supervision during VLA fine-tuning affects collision avoidance. The Detach OCC degradation ($-$3.4\,pp CAR) decomposes into BatchNorm interference (all representational drift, recovered by LayerNorm) and gradient flow absence (1.5\,pp behavioral regularization beyond representation preservation). Three label types achieve comparable CAR, suggesting gradient flow presence matters more than label content. Practical guidelines: maintain gradient flow and use per-sample normalization---no ground-truth labels needed, zero inference cost. Open directions include generalization to other auxiliary objectives, VLA architectures, dynamic obstacles, and real-world deployment.
6

1	
2	\section{Method}
3	\label{sec:method}
4	
5	We fine-tune OpenVLA-7B~\cite{kim2024openvla} with the OFT recipe~\cite{kim2025oft}, which concatenates DINOv2~\cite{oquab2024dinov2} (1024-d) and SigLIP~\cite{zhai2023siglip} (1152-d) patch features into a 2176-dimensional representation per spatial token. We apply LoRA~\cite{hu2022lora} (rank 32, $\alpha{=}16$) and augment the action prediction loss with an auxiliary spatial prediction head:
6	\begin{equation}
7	\mathcal{L}_\text{total} = \mathcal{L}_\text{action} + \lambda(t) \cdot \mathcal{L}_\text{aux}(h(\phi_v(o_t)), s_t^*),
8	\quad \lambda(t) = 0.01 \cdot \exp(-t / 50000)
9	\label{eq:total_loss}
10	\end{equation}
11	where $\lambda(t)$ decays exponentially so that spatial gradients shape early representations without dominating late-stage action refinement. The auxiliary head is discarded at inference.
12	
13	\textbf{BEV occupancy head.} The head (1.2M parameters) maps the first 256 patch tokens to a $64{\times}64$ binary occupancy grid: a linear projection ($2176{\to}256$) reshaped to $16{\times}16$, two transposed convolutions ($256{\to}128{\to}64$, kernel 4, stride 2) with BatchNorm and GELU, and a $1{\times}1$ convolution with sigmoid. Ground-truth grids are rendered from MuJoCo state replay. Training uses focal loss~\cite{lin2017focal} ($\alpha{=}0.25$, $\gamma{=}2.0$).
14	
15	\textbf{Monocular depth head.} Same architecture with ReLU output and smooth-L1 loss against egocentric depth maps rendered at $64{\times}64$ from MuJoCo. This head provides an egocentric spatial reference frame, complementing the allocentric BEV occupancy.
16	
17	\subsection{Ablation Design}
18	\label{sec:ablation}
19	
20	We evaluate eight conditions that systematically vary gradient flow, label content, spatial reference frame, and temporal extent:
21	
22	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
23	\item \textbf{Vanilla}: action loss only (baseline).
24	\item \textbf{Full OCC}: BEV occupancy with ground-truth labels; gradients flow throug

10	\begin{comment} % --- SCENARIO A START ---
11	
12	A discriminating test replaces the auxiliary head's BatchNorm2d layers with LayerNorm, which normalizes per-sample and does not maintain cross-batch running statistics, while keeping gradient detachment (\texttt{stop\_gradient}) unchanged. Detach+LN (3 seeds) achieves CAR of [DETACH_LN_CAR] ($\pm$ [DETACH_LN_STD]), recovering to within [DELTA]pp of Vanilla (0.780) and statistically indistinguishable from the no-auxiliary baseline. This confirms that the Detach degradation is a normalization artifact: BatchNorm's running statistics create forward-pass interference that the encoder cannot compensate for without auxiliary gradients. When this interference is removed via LayerNorm, the encoder performs comparably to Vanilla even without auxiliary gradient flow.
13	
14	This result refines the gradient-flow finding. Gradient flow is not inherently beneficial for collision avoidance---it is necessary specifically to compensate for the distributional perturbation that BatchNorm layers introduce in the auxiliary head. The practical implication is twofold: (1)~if practitioners use auxiliary heads with BatchNorm during VLA fine-tuning, gradient flow through the encoder is mandatory to avoid degradation; (2)~if the auxiliary head uses per-sample normalization (LayerNorm, GroupNorm), gradient detachment is safe and does not harm collision avoidance. This normalization-dependent finding explains why all gradient-flow conditions (Full OCC, Random OCC, Depth) achieve comparable CAR r

{
  "text": "## 审查报告 — cutoff_45k_s0_eval + dose-response 综合分析\n\n**结论**: WARNING（dose-response 非单调性由单一 hard task 驱动，叙事需谨慎）\n\n### cutoff_45k_s0_eval 结果\n- [severity: info] TSR=0.896, CAR=**0.764**, EXIT_CODE=0。Per-task:\n  - T0=0.98, T1=0.96, T2=0.92, **T3=0.18**, T4=0.92, **T5=0.08**, **T6=0.68**, T7=0.98, T8=0.98, T9=0.96\n\n### Dose-response 综合分析（单 seed = seed 42）\n\n| 条件 | 梯度流 | BN-only 模式 | CAR | Seeds |\n|------|--------|-------------|-----|-------|\n| Detach | 0K | 50K | 0.746 | 3 |\n| Cutoff@15K | 15K | 35K | **0.804** | 1 |\n| Cutoff@25K | 25K | 25K | 0.688 | 1 |\n| Cutoff@35K | 35K | 15K | 0.779 | 3 |\n| Cutoff@45K | 45K | 5K | 0.764 | 1 |\n| Full OCC | 50K | 0K | 0.797 | 3 |\n\n- [severity: warning] **Dose-response 严重非单调**：15K (0.804) > Full OCC (0.797) > 35K (0.779) > 45K (0.764) > Detach (0.746) > 25K (0.688)。无明确的梯度流时长与 CAR 的单调关系。\n\n### 根因：T3 主导所有方差\n\n| 条件 | T3 CAR | T6 CAR | 备注 |\n|------|--------|--------|------|\n| Cutoff@15K | **0.74** | 0.44 | T3 最高 → overall 最高 |\n| Cutoff@45K | **0.18** | 0.68 | T3 最低 → overall 偏低 |\n\n- T3 单独贡献 (0.74−0.18)/10 = **5.6pp** 差异（overall 差异仅 4.0pp，T6 反方向补偿 2.4pp）\n- T3 已知高方差（Depth 跨种子范围 0.20–0.78），单 seed 不可靠\n- **T3 和 T6 呈反相关**——cutoff timing 似乎在 hard tasks 间产生 trade-off，而非全局改善\n\n### 对论文叙事的影响\n- [severity: warning] 当前论文 §4.5 的 temporal dynamics 叙事（\"withdrawal pattern\"）基于 cutoff@25K 和 reverse_cutoff 各 1 seed。新增 15K 和 45K 数据进一步证明 **单 seed cutoff 数据方差过大，不支持任何确定性 dose-response 叙事**。\n- Cutoff@35K（3 seeds, 0.779±0.016）是唯一统计可靠的 cutoff 数据点。\n- **建议**：论文中仅保留 Cutoff@35K（3 seeds）作为主要 temporal 证据，其余 cutoff 点标为 preliminary/single-seed，或等待 cutoff_25k_s1 / reverse_cutoff_s1 eval 后再做判断。\n\n### GPU 利用率\n- [severity: warning] westd-34685 GPU 0 和 GPU 1 当前接近空闲（eval 进程残留但无训练负载）。仅 GPU 2/3 有活跃训练。建议尽快利用空闲 GPU 启动 cutoff_15k_s1、cutoff_45k_s1 或其他补充实验。\n\n### 当前实验全景\n- westd-34685: cutoff_25k_s1 + reverse_cutoff_s1（~3h 后完成）\n- westd-10894: full_occ_ln ×3 + ablation_occ_detach_s0_retrain（~10h 后完成）\n- 下一个 eval 数据产出：cutoff_25k_s1 和 reverse_cutoff_s1 完成后（~4-5h）",

}

T0=0.98, T1=0.96, T2=0.92, **T3=0.18**, T4=0.92, **T5=0.08**, **T6=0.68**, T7=0.98, T8=0.98, T9=0.96\n\n### Dose-response 综合分析（单 seed = seed 42）\n\n| 条件 | 梯度流 | BN-only 模式 | CAR | Seeds |\n|------|--------|-------------|-----|-------|\n| Detach | 0K | 50K | 0.746 | 3 |\n| Cutoff@15K | 15K | 35K | **0.804** | 1 |\n| Cutoff@25K | 25K | 25K | 0.688 | 1 |\n| Cutoff@35K | 35K | 15K | 0.779 | 3 |\n| Cutoff@45K | 45K | 5K | 0.764 | 1 |\n| Full OCC | 50K | 0K | 0.797 | 3 |\n\n- [severity: warning] **Dose-response 严重非单调**：15K (0.804) > Full OCC (0.797) > 35K (0.779) > 45K (0.764) > Detach (0.746) > 25K (0.688)。无明确的梯度流时长与 CAR 的单调关系。\n\n### 根因：T3 主导所有方差\n\n| 条件 | T3 CAR | T6 CAR | 备注 |\n|------|--------|--------|------|\n| Cutoff@15K | **0.74** | 0.44 | T3 最高 → overall 最高 |\n| Cutoff@45K | **0.18** | 0.68 | T3 最低 → overall 偏低 |\n\n- T3 单独贡献 (0.74−0.18)/10 = **5.6pp** 差异（overall 差异仅 4.0pp，T6 反方向补偿 2.4pp）\n- T3 已知高方差（Depth 跨种子范围 0.20–0.78），单 seed 不可靠\n- **T3 和 T6 呈反相关**——cutoff timing 似乎在 hard tasks 间产生 trade-off，而非全局改善\n\n### 对论文叙事的影响\n- [severity: warning] 当前论文 §4.5 的 temporal dynamics 叙事（\"withdrawal pattern\"）基于 cutoff@25K 和 reverse_cutoff 各 1 seed。新增 15K 和 45K 数据进一步证明 **单 seed cutoff 数据方差过大，不支持任何确定性 dose-response 叙事**。\n- Cutoff@35K（3 seeds, 0.779±0.016）是唯一统计可靠的 cutoff 数据点。\n- **建议**：论文中仅保留 Cutoff@35K（3 seeds）作为主要 temporal 证据，其余 cutoff 点标为 preliminary/single-seed，或等待 cutoff_25k_s1 / reverse_cutoff_s1 eval 后再做判断。\n\n### GPU 利用率\n- [severity: warning] westd-34685 GPU 0 和 GPU 1 当前接近空闲（eval 进程残留但无训练负载）。仅 GPU 2/3 有活跃训练。建议尽快利用空闲 GPU 启动 cutoff_15k_s1、cutoff_45k_s1 或其他补充实验。\n\n### 当前实验全景\n- westd-34685: cutoff_25k_s1 + reverse_cutoff_s1（~3h 后完成）\n- westd-10894: full_occ_ln ×3 + ablation_occ_detach_s0_retrain（~10h 后完成）\n- 下一个 eval 数据产出：cutoff_25k_s1 和 reverse_cutoff_s1 完成后（~4-5h）",
    "project": "occsafe_vla"
  }
}
